# Level readiness: levels 2–15

Generated by `python3 tools/level_readiness.py --write`. Native arm64 macOS; only local metadata is read. No game images, instruction listings, captures or assets are embedded.

## Evidence rules

- **L — already live:** the current first/second-level census cites native route evidence. Reuse still needs a comparison in the new area; L does not certify that new route.
- **V — translated and oracle-verified:** an existing census ledger cites executed original-instruction comparisons, or a passing audit receipt matches the current native sources and local test dependencies. Receipt-backed results are linked in the function table; older ledger evidence remains historical.
- **U — translated but unverified here:** a definition exists, but this map has not accepted execution evidence for it. An explicit oracle entry is listed separately. Some have historical full sweeps in their module docs; step 2 must audit/rerun them before promotion.
- **M — missing:** no definition or reviewed owner mapping was found. This is a conservative audit queue, not proof that no implementation exists. Dynamic entry tables and macro/alias owners need review.
- **Native boundary:** retained SDK/driver classification, excluded from the game-translation backlog. Its substitute is not an original translation; a boundary never gains V from a hook or a range label.

Function keys include the resident overlay. All census `functions` rows are used, not just `new_functions`. Combined census reports are filtered by actual beat membership; repeated rows are unioned. Census frames are not fork recording frames. Boot hits in a transition beat cannot be assigned to one subarea without per-hit residency: each such beat retains all observed scenes, rather than inventing a function/subarea attribution. The JSON report retains every beat and source.

## Ranked work

1. Audit the U/M queue and reuse the existing original-instruction harnesses. The level 8–14 harnesses already contain entry tables, original-image identity checks, ordered call/store comparisons and coverage assertions. Their presence alone does not promote a row. Keep additions opt-in and unwired.
2. Complete per-area delivery exports: object/world texture catalogs and shadow receivers beyond AREA00/AREA01, complete module packs, and AREA03 assets. Check later reload/event captures as well as each exporter’s original fixture set. Historical docs have stale gaps resolved by later profiles.
3. Measure every catalog and registry before choosing capacity. AREA00 requires 579 texture keys against 512 slots; never truncate. The allowed graphics change needs O(1) lookup plus unchanged default/GPU first-level and AREA01 tick logs, rand counters/state, presented-frame hashes, and newgame-control 9.599849. The combined SFX registry also needs a size audit.
4. Restore the missing fork route chain from a04b_00, then record later groups. Existing legacy captures remain original evidence; fork file presence alone is not parity or successful replay. Record displayed fields and missing boundary counters where needed; use the shared run lock.
5. Continue AREA00 arrival at `D_00248290` / `0015AB00` after capacity proof, then neutral frames, movement, camera, drawing, interactions and both returns. Preserve the strict AREA01 prerequisite voice discrepancy and every first divergent row. See [LEVEL3_BINDING.md](../docs/LEVEL3_BINDING.md).

These are exporter/loader inventories, not a claim that this private worktree has exported or verified them. Run the named reference test after export. The rows below preserve each asset document’s output contract; old refusal notes must be reconciled with newer bindings. Descriptive JSON/scene/material sidecars are not byte-for-byte runtime proof.

### AREA00 export profile

[AREA00_ASSETS.md](../docs/AREA00_ASSETS.md)

Exporters: [export_area00_common.py](../tools/export_area00_common.py), [export_area00_level.py](../tools/export_area00_level.py), [export_area00_sfx.py](../tools/export_area00_sfx.py), [export_area00_shadow_receivers.py](../tools/export_area00_shadow_receivers.py), [export_area00_tables.py](../tools/export_area00_tables.py), [export_area00_world_textures.py](../tools/export_area00_world_textures.py).

Reference: [test_area00_assets_reference.py](../tools/test_area00_assets_reference.py). historical capture comparisons; not rerun in this worktree.


The exports use the formats the port already reads for AREA11 and AREA01:

| Output (`assets/area00/`) | What | Port loader |
|---|---|---|
| `sub<N>/level/00_f06_id44.emdl` (+ `.gsmat.json`, descriptive) | level geometry, texels, GS state codes of sub N | `em_model_load` |
| `sub<N>/level/static_bank.emsc` | the static bank `*D_0028A5A0` of sub N | `em_script_image_load` |
| `area00.emcl` (+ `scene.txt` from export_collision.py) | grid collision with node class, rank and axis sections | `em_collision_load` |
| `area00_cells.bin` | the cell directory `*0x70003250` (disc rest state) | `em_actor_cells_load`, which **rejects** it (finding 3) |
| `sub<N>/roster.emro` | placements 0x82BB50 + deferred group 0x826F80 (EMRO header carries the sub) | `em_actor_roster_load` |
| `spawn_table.emsp` | the global spawn export; its area 0 rows are 0x24AA50 (sub 0) and 0x24ACC0 (sub 1) | `em_spawn_table_load` / `_read` |
| `door_destinations.emsp` | EMSP windows: `D_0024E140[0..0x17)` and the area 0 row 0x24DF80 (8 records) | `em_spawn_table_load` / `_read` |
| `area00_scripts/scripts.emsc` | the script window 0x8284E0..0x82BB50 (14 chains, 183 records) | `em_script_image_load` |
| `overlay_data.emsc` | the overlay data section 0x826F80..0x82D480 | `em_script_image_load` |
| `message_data.emmd` | EMMD v1 for area 0: 54 global and 54 area records, 36 stream rows, both banks | `em_message_live_install`, which **rejects** area 0 (finding 2) |
| `world_models.emwm` + `.json` | the model bank `*D_0028A59C` (39 models, 1,182,656 bytes) | `em_world_models_parse` |
| `sfx/area00_banks.bin`, `sfx/banks.json` | the area's SShd container (3 banks, 567,328 bytes) | (the registry's source) |
| `sub<N>/sfx/sfx_registry.emsr` + `.json` | EMSR v2 for scope (0, N) | `em_sfx_registry_load` |
| `sub<N>/level/level.json`, `cells.json`, `tables.json` | counts, hashes and the verification summary (no text, no data) | none |

There is no `dynamic_objects.emsc` and no `background.embg`, for the reasons
under "Level geometry" and "Background and fog" below.

The checker does not check the descriptive side files: `scene.txt`, the
`.gsmat.json` files, `world_models.json`, `sfx_registry.json`, `banks.json`,
`level.json`. It does compare these recorded lists with its own results:
- in `cells.json`: the moved hulls per capture, the 0019C6F0 calls per
  capture, and whether `moved_uids` is the union of the per-capture rows;
- in `tables.json`: the run-time words of each capture.

| `overlay_data.emsc` | the whole overlay data section 0x828A00..0x82CD00 (groups, nest group, scripts, both placement tables) | `em_script_image_load` |

| `world_models.emwm` + `.json` | the model bank `*D_0028A59C` (22 models, 288,448 bytes) | `em_world_models_parse` |

| `level/level.json`, `tables.json` | counts, hashes and the verification summary (no text, no data) | none |

**Exported but not checked by the checker** (descriptive side files; the
port loads none of them, and nothing in `src/` loads the AREA01 copies
yet): `scene.txt`, every `level/*.gsmat.json` (the per-texture GS state
record; the same codes are inside the EMDLs and are checked there, but
the JSON's fields, including the RGBAQ alpha set `rgbaq_a`, are checked
only by the exporter's `write_zone`), `world_models.json`,
`sfx/sfx_registry.json`, `sfx/banks.json`, `tables.json`, and
`level/level.json` except its `cells.moved_uids`, which the checker
compares.

binaries, every differing word with both values) and the seven differing

`sub0/world_models.emwm`, the five reports). The full export lies in
`build/area13x/assets/tree/` until it is deleted (it is regenerated by the
exporters). Port loaders as in AREA13_ASSETS.md "Outputs".

`assets/area19s1/` (ignored, about 9 MB), in `assets/area19/`'s layout:

- `sub1/`: `level/00_id44.emdl` (+ `.gsmat.json`), `level/static_bank.emsc`,
  `level/level.json`, `area19.emcl` (+ `scene.txt`), `area19_cells.bin`,
  `cells.json`, `roster.emro`, `message_data.emmd`, `world_models.emwm`
  (+ `.json`), `sfx/area19_banks.bin`, `sfx/banks.json`,
  `sfx/sfx_registry.emsr` (+ `.json`);
- `tables.json` and `loaded_sub_proof.json` (they describe the a19c
  captures);
- `sub1.json`: per `sub1/` file its SHA-256; the four side files
  (`spawn_table.emsp`, `door_destinations.emsp`, `scripts.emsc`,
  `overlay_data.emsc`) are byte-identical to `assets/area19/`'s and are
  recorded by hash, not copied; and which `sub1/` files equal a sub-0 file
  (`message_data.emmd` and `scene.txt` = `assets/area19/sub0/`'s,
  `world_models.emwm` = `assets/area19/reload/sub0/`'s: the model bank is
  the top block's, at the second load's base 0x133C1C0).

**Not `assets/area19/sub1/`** (the lane's brief named that folder): the
AREA19X lane's split and checker (`export_area19x_split.tree_files`,
`test_area19x_assets_reference.split_problems`) require every file of
`assets/area19/` outside `reload/` to be a sub-0 file its `reload.json`
lists, so a `sub1/` folder there makes both fail; this lane may not edit
them. Moving the folder needs `tree_files` to skip `sub1` as it skips
`reload` (one word in that lane's file) and the default `OUT` here changed.

Contents: level 984 bank objects -> one zone EMDL (983 objects, 12,724
vertices, 7,126 triangles, 59 textures); the grid `chunk23.n1/id42` (1,233
vertices, 644 polygons); the cell directory 64 uids, 76,648 bytes; the
roster 57 placements and one group of 21; doors used: [49] id 3, [50] id 6,
[51] id 7, [52] id 8 (door row 0x24E0F0 x10); 18 chains / 186 records;
96 area / 54 global message records; 48 world models (901,072 bytes); the
sound container (nested block 1 table entry 0, 714,448 bytes, 4 banks),
bindings as in sub 0 (group 3 bank 0 refused, no SShd magic), registry
1,000 ids: 485 audible, 506 originally absent, 9 unsupported, 66 samples.
Port loaders as in AREA13_ASSETS.md "Outputs".

| `sub0/message_data.emmd` | EMMD v1 for area 0x16: 54 global records, 0 area records, 36 stream rows, the global bank, no area bank | `em_message_live_install`, which **refuses** it (finding 2) |
| `sub0/world_models.emwm` + `.json` | the model bank `*D_0028A59C` (15 models) | `em_world_models_parse` |
| `sub0/sfx/area22_banks.bin`, `banks.json` | the area SShd container | (the registry's source) |
| `sub0/sfx/sfx_registry.emsr` + `.json` | EMSR v2 for scope (0x16, 0) | `em_sfx_registry_load` |
| `spawn_table.emsp` | the global spawn export; its area 0x16 rows are 0x24D4E0 (6 records) | `em_spawn_table_load` / `_read` |
| `door_destinations.emsp` | EMSP windows: `D_0024E140[0..0x17)` and the area 0x16 row 0x24E130 (4 records) | `em_spawn_table_load` / `_read` |
| `overlay_data.emsc` | the overlay data section 0x823600..0x823E00 | `em_script_image_load` |
| `sub0/level/level.json`, `sub0/cells.json`, `tables.json` | counts, hashes and the verification summary (no text, no data) | none |

There is no dynamic-list file, no scripts file and no `background.embg`
(sections below). The files sit under `sub0/` so that the layout matches the
earlier areas; AREA22 has one sub-state.

The checker does not check the descriptive side files: `scene.txt`, the
`.gsmat.json` files, `world_models.json`, `sfx_registry.json`, `banks.json`,
`level.json`. It does compare the recorded lists in `cells.json` (the moved
hulls per capture) and in `tables.json` (no script chain, no overlay-data
run-time word) with its own results.


## Capacity blockers

| Resource | Current capacity | Measured requirement available before this work | Work remaining |
|---|---:|---|---|
| Object textures | 512 (`src/em_gfx.h`) | AREA11 487; AREA01 460; AREA00 sub 0/1 579 each (LEVEL3_BINDING) | Export every later catalog; choose maximum; allowed O(1) registry change and regression proof |
| SFX entries / samples | 4096 / 1024 (`em_sfx_bank.c`) | AREA11/01/00 combined 3278 / 234 (LEVEL3_BINDING) | Measure complete scopes, unsupported cases and samples for every later area; capacity changes require main |
| Actor records | 256 (`em_actor_pool.h`) | No per-area peak measured here | Measure live high-water counts; never equate placement count with peak spawned actors |
| Other fixed pools | Not audited yet | Unknown | Inventory owner/model/collision/effect capacities during asset work |

## Capture limits still requiring work

- The retained legacy censuses are route-shape coverage. Some census replays differ from their recordings, and overlay swaps can leave blind spots. No full fork census is inferred from a route trace.
- a04b_00 is not a completed fork beat. Later groups have legacy traces but no fork chain. The earlier fork chain diverges from the legacy path at a02_02 after disc timing differences.
- Level 10 has unrecorded between-beat counters; levels 11 and 12 also record boundary gaps. The level-12 overlay blind spot is supplemented by a19b_swap_delta and a19b_area13_delta (level 13’s documented remeasurement).
- AREA03 has arrival and lift coverage only; its exit and subareas 1/2 are outside the retained level-15 route. Unvisited subareas of other areas and unplayed branches remain unverified.
- Per-row game state, endpoint RAM/GS, displayed fields, audio and census coverage are different proofs. A saved GS image alone does not prove the displayed framebuffer. Later live pixels and SPU residency remain separate capture work.

## Requests for main

No shared or protected source change is part of this map. Concrete diffs and regressions will be recorded here if later work needs a change outside the allowed texture-capacity exception. Known prerequisites: AREA01’s strict voice timing discrepancy; any SFX/pool capacity change proven necessary by the complete-area measurements.

## Readiness checkpoint (2026-10-18)

Thirteen disc-free attribution/receipt tests pass, including overlay identity, hook exclusion, combined-report membership, missing evidence, and snapshot completeness. The report regenerates deterministically from the retained metadata. The staged-index native build passes with zero compiler warnings (`build/levels/readiness-index-build.log`). No native gameplay or pixel pass is claimed by this tooling-only checkpoint. The current standalone audit has 36 source-matching passing receipts; 0 receipts are stale. See [LEVEL_ORACLE_AUDIT.md](LEVEL_ORACLE_AUDIT.md) for the new translations and remaining step-2 gaps. Private exports, new captures and AREA00 continuation are still pending.

## Reproduce

```sh
python3 tools/level_readiness.py --write --json build/levels/readiness.json
python3 tools/level_readiness.py --check
python3 tools/level_readiness.py --level 15 --area AREA03/sub0
python3 tools/test_level_readiness.py
```

No emulator or exporter is run by these commands. Missing required metadata fails closed; a missing fork recording is reported explicitly. Generated assets and capture receipts belong in ignored private directories and must never be staged.
